{/* MANUAL-CONTENT-START:intro */}
[Plane](https://plane.so/) manages projects, work items, and team planning.

With the Plane block, you can:

- **Manage work**: Create and update projects, work items, states, labels, comments, and attachments.
- **Plan delivery**: Organize cycles and modules, and summarize project progress.
- **Keep knowledge current**: Manage pages and sync project work items or pages into a Sim knowledge base.
- **React to changes**: Start agents from authenticated Plane webhook events.

Create a personal access token in Plane's profile settings and enter it as the API token. API v2 is the default. For Community Edition or an older self-hosted instance, select API v1 in the advanced settings and set the instance origin, optionally including its reverse-proxy path prefix. Omit `/api/v1` and `/api/v2` from the instance URL. The block exposes core workflow actions. Supported self-hosted actions use the same operation list. Resource availability depends on the instance's edition and version.

Explicit empty strings, nulls, and empty arrays can be supplied through **Body Overrides** using the action's canonical input names. Clearing work item labels and assignees is verified through API v1 because Plane v2 can continue returning deleted relationship IDs. The update action returns the verified cleared state; subsequent v2 reads may still include those IDs. Use API v1 when reading active relationships after a clear.

For webhooks, automatic registration requires API v2. On instances using API v1, create a webhook in Plane's settings and configure its signing secret in Sim. Project-filtered legacy deletion events that omit their project cannot be matched and are skipped.

The knowledge connector reads API v1 to include full descriptions. It preserves existing documents when a listing is capped and disables automatic deletion reconciliation because offset listings can shift during concurrent changes. Project pages require an edition that exposes the pages API.

In Sim, the Plane block lets your agents triage work, plan delivery, and keep project knowledge available.
{/* MANUAL-CONTENT-END */}


## Usage Instructions

Connect Plane Cloud or a self-hosted Plane instance. API v2 is the default; choose API v1 for Community Edition or older instances. Actions with a current successor are consolidated. Unsupported version-specific inputs fail explicitly. Manage work items, projects, cycles, modules, states, labels, comments, links, attachments, pages, and webhooks. API v1 compatibility is available within supported current actions.



## Actions

### Plane Archive a project

Archive a project in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `pk` | string | Yes | The project id. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `success` | boolean | Operation completed successfully. |

### Plane Archive a work item

Archive a work item in Plane. Requires API v2.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project the work item belongs to. |
| `pk` | string | Yes | The work item's UUID. This lookup is UUID-only — a `PROJ-142` identifier here returns `404`. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `archived_at`, `assignee_ids`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `custom_fields`, `cycle_id`, `id`, `identifier`, `is_draft`, `label_ids`, `module_ids`, `name`, `parent_id`, `priority`, `project_id`, `sequence_id`, `start_date`, `state_id`, `target_date`, `type_id`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed alongside the ids: `assignees` \(the assigned users\), `cycle` \(the cycle it belongs to\), `labels` \(the applied labels\), `modules` \(the modules it belongs to\), `parent` \(its parent work item\), `state` \(the work item's state object\), `type` \(its work item type\). Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 Work- Items |
|   ↳ `id` | string | Unique identifier for the work item. This is the `\{pk\}` on every project-scoped detail route. |
|   ↳ `name` | string | Name |
|   ↳ `identifier` | string | Identifier |
|   ↳ `sequence_id` | number | Sequence id |
|   ↳ `priority` | string | Priority |
|   ↳ `state_id` | string | State id |
|   ↳ `type_id` | string | Type id |
|   ↳ `assignee_ids` | array | Assignee ids |
|   ↳ `label_ids` | array | Label ids |
|   ↳ `parent_id` | string | Parent id |
|   ↳ `start_date` | string | Start date |
|   ↳ `target_date` | string | Target date |
|   ↳ `is_draft` | boolean | Is draft |
|   ↳ `archived_at` | string | Archived at |
|   ↳ `created_at` | string | Created at |
|   ↳ `created_by_id` | string | Created by id |
|   ↳ `custom_fields` | json | Custom fields |
|   ↳ `assignees` | array | Assignees |
|     ↳ `id` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `display_name` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `avatar_url` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `email` | string | Asset Id |
|   ↳ `cycle` | json | Cycle |
|   ↳ `labels` | array | Labels |
|     ↳ `id` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `name` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `color` | string | Asset Id |
|   ↳ `modules` | array | Modules |
|   ↳ `parent` | object | Parent |
|     ↳ `id` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `name` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `sequence_id` | number | Access |
|   ↳ `state` | object | State |
|     ↳ `id` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `name` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `color` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `group` | string | Asset Id |
|   ↳ `type` | object | Type |
|     ↳ `id` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `name` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `logo_props` | json | Logo Props |
|     ↳ `is_epic` | boolean | Has Pages |
|   ↳ `cycle_id` | string | Cycle id |
|   ↳ `module_ids` | array | Module ids |
|   ↳ `project_id` | string | Project id |

### Plane Confirm a work item attachment upload

Confirm a work item attachment upload in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project the resource belongs to. Accepts the project UUID or its bare identifier, for example `ENG`. |
| `work_item_id` | string | Yes | The work item the resource hangs off. Accepts the work item UUID or its `PROJ-123` identifier. |
| `pk` | string | Yes | The attachment id. |
| `is_uploaded` | boolean | No | Whether is uploaded. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted**, not returned as `null`. `id` always comes back. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). Requestable here: `asset_url`, `attributes`, `content_type`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `id`, `is_uploaded`, `name`, `size`, `work_item_id`. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 Work- Item- Attachments |
|   ↳ `asset_url` | string | Asset url |
|   ↳ `attributes` | json | Attributes |
|   ↳ `content_type` | string | Content type |
|   ↳ `created_at` | string | Created at |
|   ↳ `created_by_id` | string | Created by id |
|   ↳ `external_id` | string | External id |
|   ↳ `external_source` | string | External source |
|   ↳ `id` | string | Unique identifier. |
|   ↳ `is_uploaded` | boolean | Is uploaded |
|   ↳ `name` | string | Name |
|   ↳ `size` | json | Size |
|   ↳ `work_item_id` | string | Work item id |
| `success` | boolean | Operation completed successfully. |

### Plane Create a comment

Create a comment in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project the work item belongs to. |
| `work_item_id` | string | Yes | The work item to comment on. |
| `comment_html` | string | No | HTML comment content. Required with API v2; API v1 also supports comment_json. |
| `access` | string | No | Visibility of the comment. - `INTERNAL` — visible to the project team - `EXTERNAL` — marked as visible outside the team, for example on a published project Omit it to take the server's default. Change it later with a `PATCH`. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Your system's identifier for this comment, for sync and import correlation. Maximum 255 characters. Accepts `null`. |
| `external_source` | string | No | The system `external_id` came from, for example `github` or `zendesk`. Maximum 255 characters. Accepts `null`. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `access`, `actor_id`, `comment_html`, `comment_stripped`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `edited_at`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `id`, `work_item_id`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed alongside the ids: `actor` \(the comment author\). Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `comment_json` | json | No | v1 compatibility only. Comment json. |
| `created_at` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Creation timestamp for imports. |
| `created_by` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Original creator user ID for imports. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 Work- Item- Comments |
|   ↳ `id` | json | Id |
|   ↳ `work_item_id` | string | Work item id |
|   ↳ `comment_html` | json | Comment html |
|   ↳ `comment_stripped` | json | Comment stripped |
|   ↳ `access` | json | Access |
|   ↳ `actor_id` | string | Actor id |
|   ↳ `external_id` | string | External id |
|   ↳ `external_source` | string | External source |
|   ↳ `edited_at` | string | Edited at |
|   ↳ `created_at` | json | Created at |
|   ↳ `created_by_id` | string | Created by id |
|   ↳ `actor` | json | Actor |
|   ↳ `is_member` | boolean | Is member |
|   ↳ `updated_at` | string | Updated at |
|   ↳ `deleted_at` | string | Deleted at |
|   ↳ `attachments` | array | Attachments |
|   ↳ `created_by` | string | Created by |
|   ↳ `updated_by` | string | Updated by |
|   ↳ `project` | string | Project |
|   ↳ `workspace` | string | Workspace |
|   ↳ `issue` | string | Issue |
|   ↳ `parent` | string | Parent |
|   ↳ `description` | string | Description |
|   ↳ `comment_json` | object | Comment json |
|     ↳ `type` | string | Type |
|     ↳ `content` | array | Content |
|       ↳ `type` | string | Asset Id |
|       ↳ `content` | array | Content |
|         ↳ `type` | string | Asset Id |
|         ↳ `text` | string | Asset Id |
|   ↳ `deleted_by` | json | Deleted by |
|   ↳ `is_hidden` | boolean | Is hidden |
|   ↳ `created_via` | json | Created via |
|   ↳ `updated_via` | json | Updated via |
|   ↳ `hidden_reason` | json | Hidden reason |
|   ↳ `hidden_at` | json | Hidden at |
|   ↳ `hidden_by` | json | Hidden by |
|   ↳ `source` | json | Source |

### Plane Create a cycle

Create a cycle in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project to add the cycle to. |
| `name` | string | Yes | Display name for the cycle, unique within the project. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `description` | string | No | Free-form description of what the cycle covers. |
| `start_date` | string | No | When the cycle opens, as an ISO 8601 date-time. Nullable — omit it or send `null` for an unscheduled cycle. |
| `end_date` | string | No | When the cycle closes, as an ISO 8601 date-time. Nullable. |
| `timezone` | string | No | The IANA time zone the cycle's dates are interpreted in, for example `America/New_York`, `Asia/Kolkata`, `Europe/London`, or `UTC`. Set it to the team's working zone so a cycle boundary lands at local midnight instead of UTC midnight. Any value outside the IANA list is rejected with `400 invalid_request`. |
| `sort_order` | number | No | Ordering weight for the cycle within the project. Lower values sort first when you list with `?order_by=sort_order`. |
| `logo_props` | json | No | JSON blob holding the cycle's icon configuration, as written by Plane clients. Stored and returned unchanged. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Your system's identifier for this cycle, for sync and import correlation. Maximum 255 characters. You can find the cycle again later with `?external_id=` on \[List cycles\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/cycles/list-cycles). |
| `external_source` | string | No | The system `external_id` came from, for example `jira` or `linear`. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `description`, `end_date`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `id`, `logo_props`, `name`, `owned_by_id`, `sort_order`, `start_date`, `timezone`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed alongside the ids: `owned_by` \(the cycle owner\). Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `owned_by` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Cycle owner user ID; defaults to the authenticated user. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 Cycles |
|   ↳ `id` | json | Id |
|   ↳ `name` | json | Name |
|   ↳ `description` | json | Description |
|   ↳ `start_date` | string | Start date |
|   ↳ `end_date` | string | End date |
|   ↳ `timezone` | json | Timezone |
|   ↳ `owned_by_id` | string | Owned by id |
|   ↳ `sort_order` | json | Sort order |
|   ↳ `logo_props` | json | Logo props |
|   ↳ `external_id` | string | External id |
|   ↳ `external_source` | string | External source |
|   ↳ `created_at` | json | Created at |
|   ↳ `created_by_id` | string | Created by id |
|   ↳ `owned_by` | json | Owned by |
|   ↳ `total_issues` | json | Total issues |
|   ↳ `cancelled_issues` | json | Cancelled issues |
|   ↳ `completed_issues` | json | Completed issues |
|   ↳ `started_issues` | json | Started issues |
|   ↳ `unstarted_issues` | json | Unstarted issues |
|   ↳ `backlog_issues` | json | Backlog issues |
|   ↳ `total_estimates` | number | Total estimates |
|   ↳ `completed_estimates` | number | Completed estimates |
|   ↳ `started_estimates` | number | Started estimates |
|   ↳ `updated_at` | string | Updated at |
|   ↳ `deleted_at` | string | Deleted at |
|   ↳ `view_props` | json | View props |
|   ↳ `progress_snapshot` | json | Progress snapshot |
|   ↳ `archived_at` | string | Archived at |
|   ↳ `version` | json | Version |
|   ↳ `created_by` | string | Created by |
|   ↳ `updated_by` | string | Updated by |
|   ↳ `project` | string | Project |
|   ↳ `workspace` | string | Workspace |
|   ↳ `status` | string | Status |
|   ↳ `deleted_by` | json | Deleted by |

### Plane Create a label

Create a label in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project to add the label to. |
| `name` | string | Yes | Display name for the label, unique within the project. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `color` | string | No | Hex color used wherever the label is rendered, for example `#e5484d`. Maximum 255 characters. Send one if the label needs to be recognizable at a glance on a board. |
| `description` | string | No | Free-form note about what the label is for and when to apply it. |
| `parent_id` | string | No | The label this one nests under, for grouping related tags such as `Billing` and `Search` beneath an `Area` label. The parent must be a label in the same project — an id from another project is a `400`, not a silent link. Omit it, or send `null`, for a top-level label. |
| `sort_order` | number | No | Ordering weight within the project. Lower values sort first. Assigned automatically when omitted, so send it only when you are recreating a specific order. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Your system's identifier for this label, for sync and import correlation. Maximum 255 characters. Store it here and you can find the label later with `?external_id=`, without keeping a map of Plane ids. |
| `external_source` | string | No | The system `external_id` came from, for example `github` or `jira`. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `color`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `description`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `id`, `name`, `parent_id`, `sort_order`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 Labels |
|   ↳ `id` | json | Id |
|   ↳ `name` | json | Name |
|   ↳ `description` | json | Description |
|   ↳ `color` | json | Color |
|   ↳ `sort_order` | json | Sort order |
|   ↳ `parent_id` | string | Parent id |
|   ↳ `external_id` | string | External id |
|   ↳ `external_source` | string | External source |
|   ↳ `created_at` | json | Created at |
|   ↳ `created_by_id` | string | Created by id |
|   ↳ `updated_at` | string | Updated at |
|   ↳ `deleted_at` | string | Deleted at |
|   ↳ `created_by` | string | Created by |
|   ↳ `updated_by` | string | Updated by |
|   ↳ `workspace` | string | Workspace |
|   ↳ `project` | string | Project |
|   ↳ `parent` | string | Parent |
|   ↳ `deleted_by` | json | Deleted by |

### Plane Create a link

Create a link in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project the resource belongs to. Accepts the project UUID or its bare identifier, for example `ENG`. |
| `work_item_id` | string | Yes | The work item the resource hangs off. Accepts the work item UUID or its `PROJ-123` identifier. |
| `url` | string | Yes | Target URL. |
| `metadata` | json | No | JSON metadata object for the link. API v2 only. |
| `title` | string | No | Title. Maximum 255 characters. Nullable. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted**, not returned as `null`. `id` always comes back. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). Requestable here: `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `id`, `metadata`, `title`, `url`, `work_item_id`. |
| `created_by` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Original creator user ID for imports. |
| `issue_id` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Work item ID for the link. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 Work- Item- Links |
|   ↳ `created_at` | json | Created at |
|   ↳ `created_by_id` | string | Created by id |
|   ↳ `id` | json | Id |
|   ↳ `metadata` | json | Metadata |
|   ↳ `title` | json | Title |
|   ↳ `url` | json | Url |
|   ↳ `work_item_id` | string | Work item id |
|   ↳ `updated_at` | string | Updated at |
|   ↳ `deleted_at` | string | Deleted at |
|   ↳ `created_by` | string | Created by |
|   ↳ `updated_by` | string | Updated by |
|   ↳ `project` | string | Project |
|   ↳ `workspace` | string | Workspace |
|   ↳ `issue` | string | Issue |
|   ↳ `deleted_by` | json | Deleted by |

### Plane Create a module

Create a module in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project to create the module in. |
| `name` | string | Yes | Display name for the module, unique within the project. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `description` | string | No | Plain-text summary of what the module covers. |
| `status` | string | No | Where the module sits in its lifecycle. - `backlog` — Captured, not yet committed to - `planned` — Committed to but not started - `in-progress` — Actively being worked on - `paused` — Started, then put on hold - `completed` — Delivered - `cancelled` — Dropped without delivering Defaults to `planned` when omitted. A value outside this list is a `400 invalid_request`. |
| `start_date` | string | No | Date the module is scheduled to begin, as `YYYY-MM-DD`. Nullable. |
| `target_date` | string | No | Date the module is expected to land, as `YYYY-MM-DD`. Nullable, and must not be earlier than `start_date`. |
| `lead_id` | string | No | The user accountable for the module. Must be a member of this project — any other user id is rejected with a `400` naming `lead_id`, never linked silently. Nullable. |
| `sort_order` | number | No | Ordering weight used when modules are listed. Lower values sort first. In a project that already has modules, Plane positions the new module ahead of them on create, so send `sort_order` in a follow-up `PATCH` if you need a specific slot. |
| `logo_props` | json | No | Free-form JSON object holding the icon Plane renders for the module. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Your system's identifier for this module, for sync and import correlation. Maximum 255 characters. Nullable. |
| `external_source` | string | No | The system `external_id` came from, for example `github` or `jira`. Maximum 255 characters. Nullable. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `archived_at`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `description`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `id`, `lead_id`, `logo_props`, `member_ids`, `name`, `sort_order`, `start_date`, `status`, `target_date`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed alongside the ids: `lead` \(the module lead\), `members` \(the module members\). Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `members` | json | No | v1 compatibility only. Members. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 Modules |
|   ↳ `id` | json | Id |
|   ↳ `name` | json | Name |
|   ↳ `description` | json | Description |
|   ↳ `status` | json | Status |
|   ↳ `start_date` | string | Start date |
|   ↳ `target_date` | string | Target date |
|   ↳ `lead_id` | string | Lead id |
|   ↳ `member_ids` | array | Member ids |
|   ↳ `sort_order` | json | Sort order |
|   ↳ `logo_props` | json | Logo props |
|   ↳ `external_id` | string | External id |
|   ↳ `external_source` | string | External source |
|   ↳ `archived_at` | string | Archived at |
|   ↳ `created_at` | json | Created at |
|   ↳ `created_by_id` | string | Created by id |
|   ↳ `lead` | json | Lead |
|   ↳ `members` | array | Members |
|   ↳ `total_issues` | json | Total issues |
|   ↳ `cancelled_issues` | json | Cancelled issues |
|   ↳ `completed_issues` | json | Completed issues |
|   ↳ `started_issues` | json | Started issues |
|   ↳ `unstarted_issues` | json | Unstarted issues |
|   ↳ `backlog_issues` | json | Backlog issues |
|   ↳ `updated_at` | string | Updated at |
|   ↳ `deleted_at` | string | Deleted at |
|   ↳ `description_text` | json | Description text |
|   ↳ `description_html` | json | Description html |
|   ↳ `view_props` | json | View props |
|   ↳ `created_by` | string | Created by |
|   ↳ `updated_by` | string | Updated by |
|   ↳ `project` | string | Project |
|   ↳ `workspace` | string | Workspace |
|   ↳ `deleted_by` | json | Deleted by |

### Plane Create a project

Create a project in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `identifier` | string | Yes | Short project key used to prefix work item numbers, for example `ENG` in `ENG-142`. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `name` | string | Yes | Display name. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `archive_in` | number | No | The archive in. |
| `close_in` | number | No | The close in. |
| `cover_image` | string | No | URL of the cover image. Nullable. |
| `cycle_view` | boolean | No | Whether cycle view. |
| `default_assignee_id` | string | No | Id of the related default assignee. Nullable. |
| `default_state_id` | string | No | Id of the related default state. Nullable. |
| `description` | string | No | Free-form description. |
| `emoji` | string | No | Emoji shown alongside the name. Maximum 255 characters. Nullable. |
| `estimate_id` | string | No | Id of the related estimate. Nullable. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Your system's identifier for this record, for sync and import correlation. Maximum 255 characters. Nullable. |
| `external_source` | string | No | The system `external_id` came from, for example `github` or `jira`. Maximum 255 characters. Nullable. |
| `guest_view_all_features` | boolean | No | Whether guest view all features. |
| `icon_prop` | string | No | The icon prop. Nullable. |
| `intake_view` | boolean | No | Whether intake view. |
| `is_issue_type_enabled` | boolean | No | Whether is issue type enabled. |
| `is_time_tracking_enabled` | boolean | No | Whether is time tracking enabled. |
| `issue_views_view` | boolean | No | Whether issue views view. |
| `logo_props` | string | No | Editor-owned logo descriptor. Pass back what you read rather than composing it by hand. |
| `module_view` | boolean | No | Whether module view. |
| `network` | string | No | Project visibility: `0` is private to members, `2` is visible to the whole workspace. |
| `page_view` | boolean | No | Whether page view. |
| `priority` | string | No | - `none` - None - `low` - Low - `medium` - Medium - `high` - High - `urgent` - Urgent One of `none`, `low`, `medium`, `high`, `urgent`. |
| `project_lead_id` | string | No | Id of the related project lead. Nullable. |
| `start_date` | string | No | Planned start date, as `YYYY-MM-DD`. Nullable. |
| `state_id` | string | No | Id of the related state. Nullable. |
| `target_date` | string | No | Planned due date, as `YYYY-MM-DD`. Nullable. |

| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted**, not returned as `null`. `id` always comes back. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). Requestable here: `archive_in`, `archived_at`, `close_in`, `cover_image`, `cover_image_url`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `cycle_view`, `default_assignee_id`, `default_state_id`, `description`, `emoji`, `estimate_id`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `guest_view_all_features`, `icon_prop`, `id`, `identifier`, `intake_view`, `is_issue_type_enabled`, `is_time_tracking_enabled`, `issue_views_view`, `logo_props`, `module_view`, `name`, `network`, `page_view`, `priority`, `project_lead_id`, `start_date`, `state_id`, `target_date`, `timezone`. |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed: `default_assignee`, `project_lead`. Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `v1_icon_prop` | json | No | v1 compatibility only. Icon prop. |

### Plane Create a project page

Create a project page in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project the resource belongs to. Accepts the project UUID or its bare identifier, for example `ENG`. |
| `name` | string | Yes | Display name. |
| `access` | string | No | Who can see this. |
| `archived_at` | string | No | The archived at. Nullable. |
| `collection_id` | string | No | Id of the related collection. Nullable. |
| `color` | string | No | Hex color used wherever this is rendered, for example `#3f76ff`. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `description_html` | string | No | Rich-text body as HTML. This is the field the Plane editor round-trips. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Your system's identifier for this record, for sync and import correlation. Maximum 255 characters. Nullable. |
| `external_source` | string | No | The system `external_id` came from, for example `github` or `jira`. Maximum 255 characters. Nullable. |
| `is_locked` | boolean | No | Prevents further edits to the content. |
| `logo_props` | string | No | Editor-owned logo descriptor. Pass back what you read rather than composing it by hand. |
| `parent_id` | string | No | Id of the related parent. Nullable. |
| `sort_order` | number | No | Manual ordering weight. Lower sorts first. |
| `view_props` | string | No | Editor-owned layout descriptor. Pass back what you read. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted**, not returned as `null`. `id` always comes back. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). Requestable here: `access`, `archived_at`, `collection_id`, `color`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `description_html`, `description_stripped`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `id`, `is_global`, `is_locked`, `logo_props`, `name`, `owned_by_id`, `parent_id`, `sort_order`, `view_props`. |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed: `owned_by`, `parent`. Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `v1_access` | number | No | v1 compatibility only. - `0` - Public - `1` - Private |
| `v1_view_props` | json | No | v1 compatibility only. View props. |
| `v1_logo_props` | json | No | v1 compatibility only. Logo props. |

### Plane Create a state

Create a state in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project to add the state to. |
| `name` | string | Yes | Display name for the state, unique within the project. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `color` | string | Yes | Hex color used wherever the state is rendered, for example `#3f76ff`. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `group` | string | No | The workflow group the state belongs to. Determines how the state is treated by boards, charts, and cycle progress. - `backlog` — Not yet scheduled - `unstarted` — Scheduled but not begun - `started` — Actively in progress - `completed` — Finished successfully - `cancelled` — Closed without completion - `triage` — Awaiting intake review Defaults to `backlog` when omitted. |
| `description` | string | No | Free-form description of what the state means in this workflow. |
| `sequence` | number | No | Ordering weight within the project. Lower values sort first. Assigned automatically when omitted. |
| `is_default` | boolean | No | Make this the project's default state — where work items land when no `state_id` is supplied. Setting it clears the flag on the previous default. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Your system's identifier for this state, for sync and import correlation. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `external_source` | string | No | The system `external_id` came from, for example `github` or `jira`. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `color`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `description`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `group`, `id`, `is_default`, `is_triage`, `name`, `sequence`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `is_triage` | boolean | No | v1 compatibility only. Is triage. |
| `default` | boolean | No | v1 compatibility only. Default. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 States |
|   ↳ `id` | json | Id |
|   ↳ `name` | json | Name |
|   ↳ `description` | json | Description |
|   ↳ `color` | json | Color |
|   ↳ `group` | json | Group |
|   ↳ `sequence` | json | Sequence |
|   ↳ `is_default` | boolean | Is default |
|   ↳ `is_triage` | json | Is triage |
|   ↳ `external_id` | string | External id |
|   ↳ `external_source` | string | External source |
|   ↳ `created_at` | json | Created at |
|   ↳ `created_by_id` | string | Created by id |
|   ↳ `updated_at` | string | Updated at |
|   ↳ `deleted_at` | string | Deleted at |
|   ↳ `default` | boolean | Default |
|   ↳ `created_by` | string | Created by |
|   ↳ `updated_by` | string | Updated by |
|   ↳ `project` | string | Project |
|   ↳ `workspace` | string | Workspace |
|   ↳ `slug` | string | Slug |
|   ↳ `deleted_by` | json | Deleted by |

### Plane Create a webhook

Create a webhook in Plane. Requires API v2.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `content_type` | string | No | - `application/json` - application/json - `application/x-www-form-urlencoded` - application/x-www-form-urlencoded One of `application/json`, `application/x-www-form-urlencoded`. |
| `is_active` | boolean | No | Whether the record is active. |
| `name` | string | No | Display name. Maximum 255 characters. Nullable. |
| `scopes` | json | No | The scopes. |
| `url` | string | Yes | Target URL. |
| `version` | string | No | Version string, for example `1.4.0`. Maximum 10 characters. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted**, not returned as `null`. `id` always comes back. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). Requestable here: `content_type`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `id`, `is_active`, `name`, `scopes`, `url`, `version`. |

|   ↳ `scopes` | array | Scopes |
|   ↳ `url` | string | Url |
|   ↳ `version` | string | Version |
|   ↳ `secret_key` | string | Secret key |

### Plane Create a work item

Create a work item in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project to create the work item in. |
| `name` | string | Yes | Title of the work item. Maximum 255 characters. The only required field. |
| `description_html` | string | No | Rich-text body as HTML, for example `Steps to reproduce…`. The HTML is sanitized on the way in; content that can't be sanitized is rejected with a `400`. `description_html` is **not** part of the read shape, so it will not appear in the response. |
| `priority` | string | No | One of `urgent`, `high`, `medium`, `low`, `none`. Defaults to `none`. |
| `state_id` | string | No | The workflow state to create the work item in. Must be a state of this project. Omit it and the work item lands in the project's default state. |
| `state` | string | No | The state's **name** instead of its id, for example `In Progress`. Matched case-insensitively within the project. Write-only. Unknown name or a name that matches more than one state is a `400`. Sending both `state` and `state_id` is a `400`. |
| `type_id` | string | No | The work item type. Accepts a type owned by the project and a workspace-level type imported into it. Requires work item types to be enabled. |
| `type` | string | No | The type's **name** instead of its id, for example `Bug`. Matched case-insensitively among the project's non-epic types. Write-only. |
| `parent_id` | string | No | The parent work item. The parent must be in the same workspace, but it may live in a different project. |
| `parent` | string | No | The parent's **identifier** instead of its id, for example `PROJ-118`. Resolved within the workspace. Write-only. |
| `assignee_ids` | json | No | User ids to assign. Each must be an active, assignable member of the project. Leave it out and the project's default assignee is applied, if one is configured. Send `\[\]` to create the work item with no assignee at all. |
| `assignees` | json | No | Member **email addresses** instead of ids, for example `\["ana@example.com"\]`. Every address must belong to an active, assignable project member — any that don't are named in the `400`. Write-only. |
| `label_ids` | json | No | Label ids to apply. Each must be a label of this project, or a workspace-level label available to it. |
| `labels` | json | No | Label **names** instead of ids, for example `\["regression", "auth"\]`. Matched case-insensitively against the project's labels and the workspace-level labels available to it. A name that exists at both levels is ambiguous and returns a `400` telling you to use `label_ids`. Write-only. |
| `estimate_point_id` | string | No | The estimate point to assign, from the project's active estimate system. |
| `estimate` | string | No | The estimate point's **value** instead of its id, for example `5` or `L`. Resolved against the project's active estimate. Write-only. |
| `start_date` | string | No | Planned start, for example `2026-01-12`. Must not be after `target_date`. |
| `target_date` | string | No | Planned due date, for example `2026-01-20`. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Your system's identifier for this work item, for sync and import correlation. Maximum 255 characters. Stored and filterable on \[List work items\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/work-items/list-work-items), but not returned on reads. |
| `external_source` | string | No | The system `external_id` came from, for example `github` or `jira`. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `archived_at`, `assignee_ids`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `custom_fields`, `cycle_id`, `id`, `identifier`, `is_draft`, `label_ids`, `module_ids`, `name`, `parent_id`, `priority`, `project_id`, `sequence_id`, `start_date`, `state_id`, `target_date`, `type_id`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed alongside the ids: `assignees` \(the assigned users\), `cycle` \(the cycle it belongs to\), `labels` \(the applied labels\), `modules` \(the modules it belongs to\), `parent` \(its parent work item\), `state` \(the work item's state object\), `type` \(its work item type\). Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `deleted_at` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Deleted at. |
| `point` | number | No | v1 compatibility only. Point. |
| `sequence_id` | number | No | v1 compatibility only. Sequence id. |
| `sort_order` | number | No | v1 compatibility only. Sort order. |
| `archived_at` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Archived at. |
| `is_draft` | boolean | No | v1 compatibility only. Is draft. |
| `created_by` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Created by. |
| `created_at` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Creation timestamp for imports. |
| `custom_fields` | json | No | JSON custom field values. API v2 only. |
| `cycle_id` | string | No | Cycle ID. API v2 only. |
| `module_ids` | json | No | Module IDs. API v2 only. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 Work- Items |
|   ↳ `id` | json | Id |
|   ↳ `name` | json | Name |
|   ↳ `identifier` | string | Identifier |
|   ↳ `sequence_id` | json | Sequence id |
|   ↳ `priority` | json | Priority |

|     ↳ `workspace` | string | Asset Id |

### Plane Delete an attachment

Delete an attachment in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |

| `work_item_id` | string | Yes | The work item the resource hangs off. Accepts the work item UUID or its `PROJ-123` identifier. |
| `pk` | string | Yes | The attachment id. |

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `success` | boolean | Operation completed successfully. |

| `pk` | string | Yes | The id of the state to retrieve. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `color`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `description`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `group`, `id`, `is_default`, `is_triage`, `name`, `sequence`. ### Plane Get a work item

Get a work item in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project the work item belongs to. |
| `pk` | string | Yes | The work item's UUID. This lookup is UUID-only — a `PROJ-142` identifier here returns `404`. |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed alongside the ids: `assignees` \(the assigned users\), `cycle` \(the cycle it belongs to\), `labels` \(the applied labels\), `modules` \(the modules it belongs to\), `parent` \(its parent work item\), `state` \(the work item's state object\), `type` \(its work item type\). Expansion is separate-key: `?expand=state` keeps `state_id` and adds a `state` object next to it, so an id is never replaced by an object. An unknown value is a `400`. `?fields=` and `?expand=` are independent namespaces. Relation names are not valid `?fields=` tokens \(and vice versa\), and an expanded object survives field filtering — `?fields=id,name&expand=state` returns `id`, `name` and `state`. See \[Expanding relations\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/expanding-relations). Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `archived_at`, `assignee_ids`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `custom_fields`, `cycle_id`, `id`, `identifier`, `is_draft`, `label_ids`, `module_ids`, `name`, `parent_id`, `priority`, `project_id`, `sequence_id`, `start_date`, `state_id`, `target_date`, `type_id`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `external_id` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. External system identifier for filtering or lookup |
| `external_source` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. External system source name for filtering or lookup |
| `order_by` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Field to order results by. Prefix with '-' for descending order |

### Plane Get a work item by identifier

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `identifier` | string | Yes | The work item's human key: the project identifier, a hyphen, and the work item's number — `PROJ-142`. The project part must start with a letter, and it is upper-cased before lookup, so `proj-142` resolves the same work item as `PROJ-142`. A project identifier is unique within a workspace, which is what makes this key unambiguous. |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed alongside the ids: `assignees` \(the assigned users\), `cycle` \(the cycle it belongs to\), `labels` \(the applied labels\), `modules` \(the modules it belongs to\), `parent` \(its parent work item\), `state` \(the work item's state object\), `type` \(its work item type\). Expansion is separate-key: `?expand=state` keeps `state_id` and adds a `state` object next to it, so an id is never replaced by an object. An unknown value is a `400`. `?fields=` and `?expand=` are independent namespaces. Relation names are not valid `?fields=` tokens \(and vice versa\), and an expanded object survives field filtering — `?fields=id,name&expand=state` returns `id`, `name` and `state`. See \[Expanding relations\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/expanding-relations). Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `archived_at`, `assignee_ids`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `custom_fields`, `cycle_id`, `id`, `identifier`, `is_draft`, `label_ids`, `module_ids`, `name`, `parent_id`, `priority`, `project_id`, `sequence_id`, `start_date`, `state_id`, `target_date`, `type_id`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |

### Plane List comments

List comments in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project the work item belongs to. |
| `work_item_id` | string | Yes | The work item whose comments you want. Comments never span work items, so this narrows the result set by itself. |
| `access` | string | No | Return only comments with this visibility. - `INTERNAL` — visible to the project team - `EXTERNAL` — marked as visible outside the team |
| `external_id` | string | No | Return comments carrying this identifier from your system. `external_id` is not a unique key, so this can match more than one comment — pair it with `external_source` and handle a multi-row result. |
| `external_source` | string | No | Return comments that came from this system, for example `github` or `zendesk`. |
| `search` | string | No | Match comments against their plain-text body \(`comment_stripped`\), so HTML markup in `comment_html` never affects whether a term hits. |
| `order_by` | string | No | Field to sort by. Prefix with `-` for descending. Send it explicitly whenever order matters — a thread you render should not depend on the server's unstated default. - `created_at` — oldest first - `-created_at` — newest first - `id` - `-id` |
| `per_page` | number | No | Page size. Maximum 200 for API v2 and 100 for API v1. |
| `offset` | number | No | Number of rows to skip from the start of the result set. Maximum 10000 — for deeper traversal switch to cursor pagination. |
| `paginate` | string | No | Set to `cursor` to opt into the COUNT-free keyset envelope instead of the default offset envelope. The response then carries `next_cursor` and `has_more`; send the value of `next_cursor` back as `?cursor=` to fetch the next page. See \[Pagination\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/pagination) for the full envelope. |
| `count` | boolean | No | Defaults to `true`. Set to `false` to skip the `COUNT\(*\)` and omit `total_count` from the offset envelope. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return on each row. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `access`, `actor_id`, `comment_html`, `comment_stripped`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `edited_at`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `id`, `work_item_id`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed alongside the ids: `actor` \(the comment author\). Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `cursor` | string | No | Cursor from next_cursor. Repeat the original filters, per_page, and order_by when following it. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 V2 List Commentsresult |
|   ↳ `data` | array | Data |
|     ↳ `id` | string | Unique identifier for the comment. |
|     ↳ `work_item_id` | string | The work item this comment is attached to. Always matches the `work_item_id` in the request path. |
|     ↳ `comment_html` | string | The comment body, as HTML. This is the field you write. |

|     ↳ `access` | string | Visibility of the comment. One of `INTERNAL` or `EXTERNAL`. `INTERNAL` keeps the comment inside the project team; `EXTERNAL` marks it as visible outside the team, for example on a published project. |

|     ↳ `created_by_id` | string | Asset Id |

|   ↳ `next` | number | Next |

### Plane List cycles

List cycles in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project whose cycles you want. |
| `owned_by_id` | string | No | Return only cycles owned by this user. This is how you build a "my cycles" view — pass the authenticated user's id. Ownership is assigned by Plane, so this is a read-side filter only. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Return only cycles whose `external_id` matches exactly. Pair it with `external_source` when the same identifier could come from more than one system. |
| `external_source` | string | No | Return only cycles imported from this source, for example `jira`. |
| `search` | string | No | Free-text search term matched against the cycle. |
| `order_by` | string | No | Field to sort by. Prefix with `-` for descending. - `sort_order` / `-sort_order` — the project's manual cycle ordering - `created_at` / `-created_at` — newest or oldest first - `id` / `-id` |
| `per_page` | number | No | Page size. Maximum 200 for API v2 and 100 for API v1. |
| `offset` | number | No | Number of rows to skip from the start of the result set. Maximum 10000 — past that, switch to cursor pagination. |
| `paginate` | string | No | Set to `cursor` to opt into the COUNT-free keyset envelope instead of the default offset envelope. The response then carries `next_cursor` and `has_more` rather than `next`, `previous`, and `total_count`; pass `next_cursor` back as `cursor` to walk to the following page. Use it for deep traversal, where offset paging gets expensive. |
| `count` | boolean | No | Defaults to `true`. Set to `false` to skip the `COUNT\(*\)` behind `total_count` — the field is then omitted from the envelope. Worth doing when you only need the rows. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return on each row. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `description`, `end_date`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `id`, `logo_props`, `name`, `owned_by_id`, `sort_order`, `start_date`, `timezone`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed alongside the ids: `owned_by` \(the cycle owner\). Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `cursor` | string | No | Cursor from next_cursor. Repeat the original filters, per_page, and order_by when following it. |
| `cycle_view` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Filter cycles by status |

### Plane List labels

List labels in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project whose labels you want. |
| `parent_id` | string | No | Return only the labels nested under this label. Pair it with `parent_id__isnull=true` to get the opposite view — every top-level label in the project. Use `parent_id__isnull=false` for every label that has a parent. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Return labels whose `external_id` matches. Combine with `external_source` to resolve a record from another system to its Plane label without keeping a local id map. |
| `external_source` | string | No | Return labels that came from this system, for example `github` or `jira`. |
| `search` | string | No | A search term matched against the label `name`. Use it to power type-ahead in a picker rather than downloading every page and filtering client-side. |
| `order_by` | string | No | Field to sort by. Prefix with `-` for descending. Defaults to `sort_order`. - `sort_order` , `-sort_order` — the project's own label ordering - `created_at` , `-created_at` — newest or oldest first - `id` , `-id` A value outside this list is not rejected — it silently falls back to the default `sort_order`. Check your spelling: a typo in `order_by` fails silently and shows up as a differently sorted page, not as an error. |
| `per_page` | number | No | Page size. Maximum 200 for API v2 and 100 for API v1. |
| `offset` | number | No | Number of rows to skip from the start of the result set. Maximum 10000. Read the `next` value from the response and send it back as `offset` to walk forward. |
| `paginate` | string | No | Set to `cursor` to switch from the default offset envelope to the keyset cursor envelope, which skips the `COUNT\(*\)` and returns `next_cursor` and `has_more` instead of `next` and `total_count`. Send the returned `next_cursor` back as `cursor` to fetch the following page. Only `created_at` and `id` are cursor-eligible, because a keyset needs a strictly ordered column and `sort_order` is neither unique nor monotonic. Pair `paginate=cursor` with `order_by=created_at` or `order_by=id` — the default `sort_order` ordering is rejected with `400 ordering_not_cursor_eligible`. Most projects have few enough labels that the default offset envelope is all you need. |
| `count` | boolean | No | Defaults to `true`. Set to `false` to omit `total_count` and skip the count query. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return on each row. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `color`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `description`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `id`, `name`, `parent_id`, `sort_order`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `cursor` | string | No | Cursor from next_cursor. Repeat the original filters, per_page, and order_by when following it. |
| `expand` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Comma-separated list of related fields to expand in response Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `parent_id__isnull` | boolean | No | API v2: true selects top-level labels, false selects labels with a parent. |

### Plane List modules

List modules in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project whose modules you want. |
| `status` | string | No | Return only modules in this lifecycle position. One of `backlog`, `planned`, `in-progress`, `paused`, `completed`, or `cancelled`. Use `status__in` with a comma-separated list to match several at once, for example `?status__in=planned,in-progress`. |
| `lead_id` | string | No | Return only modules led by this user. Match is exact on the module's `lead_id`. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Return only modules carrying this external identifier. Pair it with `external_source` to resolve a record you imported from another system. |
| `external_source` | string | No | Return only modules that came from this system, for example `github` or `jira`. |
| `search` | string | No | Free-text match on the module name. |
| `order_by` | string | No | Field to sort by. Prefix with `-` for descending. - `sort_order` , `-sort_order` - `created_at` , `-created_at` - `id` , `-id` Defaults to `sort_order`. |
| `per_page` | number | No | Page size. Maximum 200 for API v2 and 100 for API v1. |
| `offset` | number | No | Number of rows to skip from the start of the result set. Maximum 10000 — go deeper with cursor pagination. |
| `paginate` | string | No | Set to `cursor` to opt into the COUNT-free keyset envelope, then follow `next_cursor` with `?cursor=`. |
| `count` | boolean | No | Set to `false` to skip the count query and omit `total_count` from the offset envelope. Defaults to `true`. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return on each row. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `archived_at`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `description`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `id`, `lead_id`, `logo_props`, `member_ids`, `name`, `sort_order`, `start_date`, `status`, `target_date`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `status__in` | string | No | Documented filter variant. Return only modules in this lifecycle position. One of `backlog`, `planned`, `in-progress`, `paused`, `completed`, or `cancelled`. Use `status__in` with a comma-separated list to match several at once, for example `?status__in=planned,in-progress`. |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed alongside the ids: `lead` \(the module lead\), `members` \(the module members\). Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `cursor` | string | No | Cursor from next_cursor. Repeat the original filters, per_page, and order_by when following it. |
