### Plane List states

List states in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project whose states you want to list. |
| `group` | string | No | Return only states in this workflow group. One of `backlog`, `unstarted`, `started`, `completed`, `cancelled`, or `triage`. Use the `group__in` variant to match several groups at once, passing them comma-separated — `?group__in=started,completed`. Filtering by group is the portable way to ask "what counts as in progress here", because every project names its states differently but the groups are fixed. |
| `is_default` | boolean | No | Return only the project's default state \(`true`\) or only the non-default states \(`false`\). Pairing `?is_default=true` with `?per_page=1` is the cheapest way to find where new work items will land. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Return states whose `external_id` matches exactly. Use it to find the state you previously created for a record in another system. |
| `external_source` | string | No | Return states that came from a particular system, for example `github` or `jira`. Combine it with `external_id` — an `external_id` is only unique within its source. |
| `search` | string | No | A search term matched against the state name. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return on each row. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `color`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `description`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `group`, `id`, `is_default`, `is_triage`, `name`, `sequence`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `group__in` | string | No | Documented filter variant. Return only states in this workflow group. One of `backlog`, `unstarted`, `started`, `completed`, `cancelled`, or `triage`. Use the `group__in` variant to match several groups at once, passing them comma-separated — `?group__in=started,completed`. Filtering by group is the portable way to ask "what counts as in progress here", because every project names its states differently but the groups are fixed. |
| `order_by` | string | No | Field to sort by. Prefix with `-` for descending. - `sequence` , `-sequence` — the project's own workflow order - `created_at` , `-created_at` — when each state was added - `id` , `-id` Order by `sequence` when you are rendering the workflow to a user; it is the order the project itself uses. |
| `per_page` | number | No | Page size. Maximum 200 for API v2 and 100 for API v1. |
| `offset` | number | No | Number of rows to skip from the start of the result set. Maximum 10000. Read the `next` value from the response rather than computing offsets yourself. |
| `paginate` | string | No | Set to `cursor` to opt into the COUNT-free keyset envelope, which returns `next_cursor` and `has_more` instead of `next` and `total_count`. Omit it for the default offset envelope. |
| `count` | boolean | No | Defaults to `true`. Set to `false` to skip the `COUNT\(*\)` behind `total_count`; the field is then omitted from the response. |
| `cursor` | string | No | Cursor from next_cursor. Repeat the original filters, per_page, and order_by when following it. |
| `expand` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Comma-separated list of related fields to expand in response Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 V2 List Statesresult |
|   ↳ `data` | array | Data |
|     ↳ `id` | string | Unique identifier for the state. |
|     ↳ `name` | string | Display name, unique within the project. Maximum 255 characters. |
|     ↳ `description` | string | Free-form description of what the state means in this workflow. |
|     ↳ `color` | string | Hex color used wherever the state is rendered, for example `#3f76ff`. |
|     ↳ `group` | string | The workflow group this state belongs to. One of `backlog`, `unstarted`, `started`, `completed`, `cancelled`, or `triage`. |
|     ↳ `sequence` | json | Sequence |
|     ↳ `is_default` | boolean | Whether new work items land in this state when no `state_id` is supplied. Exactly one state per project is the default. |
|     ↳ `is_triage` | boolean | Whether this is the project's triage state, used by intake. Read-only — a triage state is created and managed by Plane. |
|     ↳ `external_id` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `external_source` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `created_at` | string | When the state was created. |
|     ↳ `created_by_id` | string | The user who created the state. |
|   ↳ `next` | number | Next |
|   ↳ `previous` | number | Previous |
|   ↳ `total_count` | number | Total count |
|   ↳ `pagination` | object | Pagination |
|     ↳ `style` | string | Style |
|   ↳ `next_cursor` | string | Next cursor |
|   ↳ `has_more` | boolean | Has more |
| `results` | array | Returned Plane records. |
|   ↳ `id` | string | Id |

| `pagination` | object | Cursor pagination information. Bare-array responses have empty pagination. |

### Plane List work items

List work items in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project to list work items from. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return on each row. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `archived_at`, `assignee_ids`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `cycle_id`, `id`, `identifier`, `is_draft`, `label_ids`, `module_ids`, `name`, `parent_id`, `priority`, `project_id`, `sequence_id`, `start_date`, `state_id`, `target_date`, `type_id`. Naming `custom_fields` here is a `400` — it is only available on single-object responses. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `state_id` | string | No | Match work items in a specific state. Use `state_id__in` with a comma-separated list to match any of several states. |
| `state_id__in` | string | No | Documented filter variant. Match work items in a specific state. Use `state_id__in` with a comma-separated list to match any of several states. |
| `state_group` | string | No | Match by the state's workflow group instead of a specific state — stable across projects that name their states differently. One of `backlog`, `unstarted`, `started`, `completed`, `cancelled`, `triage`. Use `state_group__in` for several groups, for example `?state_group__in=started,completed`. |
| `state_group__in` | string | No | Documented filter variant. Match by the state's workflow group instead of a specific state — stable across projects that name their states differently. One of `backlog`, `unstarted`, `started`, `completed`, `cancelled`, `triage`. Use `state_group__in` for several groups, for example `?state_group__in=started,completed`. |
| `priority` | string | No | One of `urgent`, `high`, `medium`, `low`, `none`. Use `priority__in` for several, for example `?priority__in=urgent,high`. |
| `priority__in` | string | No | Documented filter variant. One of `urgent`, `high`, `medium`, `low`, `none`. Use `priority__in` for several, for example `?priority__in=urgent,high`. |
| `assignee_id` | string | No | Match work items assigned to a user. `assignee_id__in` matches any of several users; `assignee_id__isnull=true` returns only unassigned work items. |
| `assignee_id__in` | string | No | Documented filter variant. Match work items assigned to a user. `assignee_id__in` matches any of several users; `assignee_id__isnull=true` returns only unassigned work items. |
| `label_id` | string | No | Match work items carrying a label. `label_id__in` matches any of several labels; `label_id__isnull=true` returns only unlabeled work items. |
| `label_id__in` | string | No | Documented filter variant. Match work items carrying a label. `label_id__in` matches any of several labels; `label_id__isnull=true` returns only unlabeled work items. |
| `type_id` | string | No | Match work items of a given work item type. `type_id__in` accepts a comma-separated list. |
| `type_id__in` | string | No | Documented filter variant. Match work items of a given work item type. `type_id__in` accepts a comma-separated list. |
| `parent_id` | string | No | Match the children of a work item. `parent_id__in` accepts several parents; `parent_id__isnull=true` returns only top-level work items. |
| `parent_id__in` | string | No | Documented filter variant. Match the children of a work item. `parent_id__in` accepts several parents; `parent_id__isnull=true` returns only top-level work items. |
| `cycle_id` | string | No | Match work items in a cycle. `cycle_id__in` accepts several cycles; `cycle_id__isnull=true` returns the backlog of work items in no cycle at all. |
| `cycle_id__in` | string | No | Documented filter variant. Match work items in a cycle. `cycle_id__in` accepts several cycles; `cycle_id__isnull=true` returns the backlog of work items in no cycle at all. |
| `module_id` | string | No | Match work items in a module. `module_id__in` accepts several modules; `module_id__isnull=true` returns work items in no module. |
| `module_id__in` | string | No | Documented filter variant. Match work items in a module. `module_id__in` accepts several modules; `module_id__isnull=true` returns work items in no module. |
| `sequence_id` | number | No | Match the single work item with this number within the project — the `142` of `PROJ-142`. |
| `is_draft` | boolean | No | Filter drafts in or out. Drafts are work items still being composed in the Plane app. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Correlation filters for sync and import. `external_source` is the system a record came from and `external_id` is its key there, so the pair is how you find the Plane work item that mirrors a row in your own database. These values are not returned on reads — the lookup is one-way. |
| `external_source` | string | No | Correlation filters for sync and import. `external_source` is the system a record came from and `external_id` is its key there, so the pair is how you find the Plane work item that mirrors a row in your own database. These values are not returned on reads — the lookup is one-way. |
| `created_at__gte` | string | No | Bound the creation timestamp. Pass an ISO 8601 datetime, for example `2026-01-01T00:00:00Z`. Use both for a window. |
| `created_at__lte` | string | No | Bound the creation timestamp. Pass an ISO 8601 datetime, for example `2026-01-01T00:00:00Z`. Use both for a window. |
| `updated_at__gte` | string | No | Bound the last-modified timestamp. This is the pair to use for incremental sync — poll with `?updated_at__gte=&order_by=updated_at`. `updated_at` is filterable and orderable but is not part of the read shape, so it does not come back in the response body. |
| `updated_at__lte` | string | No | Bound the last-modified timestamp. This is the pair to use for incremental sync — poll with `?updated_at__gte=&order_by=updated_at`. `updated_at` is filterable and orderable but is not part of the read shape, so it does not come back in the response body. |
| `start_date__gte` | string | No | Bound the planned start date, for example `2026-01-01`. |
| `start_date__lte` | string | No | Bound the planned start date, for example `2026-01-01`. |
| `target_date__gte` | string | No | Bound the planned due date. `?target_date__lte=2026-01-31&state_group__in=backlog,unstarted,started` is the "what is about to slip" query. |
| `target_date__lte` | string | No | Bound the planned due date. `?target_date__lte=2026-01-31&state_group__in=backlog,unstarted,started` is the "what is about to slip" query. |
| `search` | string | No | Free-text search over the work item name. |
| `order_by` | string | No | Field to sort by. Prefix with `-` for descending. Defaults to `-created_at`. - `created_at`, `-created_at` - `updated_at`, `-updated_at` - `sequence_id`, `-sequence_id` - `id`, `-id` - `sort_order`, `-sort_order` — the manual board ordering - `priority`, `-priority` — semantic: `urgent` → `high` → `medium` → `low` → `none`, not alphabetical - `state_group`, `-state_group` — semantic: workflow order, not alphabetical `priority` and `state_group` sort by meaning, which is what you want for a board but is not cursor-eligible. Pair either with `?paginate=cursor` and you get `400 ordering_not_cursor_eligible`; use the default offset pages instead. |
| `per_page` | number | No | Page size. Maximum 200 for API v2 and 100 for API v1. |
| `offset` | number | No | Rows to skip from the start of the result set. Maximum 10000 — past that, switch to cursor pagination. |
| `count` | boolean | No | Defaults to `true`. Pass `?count=false` to skip the `COUNT\(*\)` and omit `total_count` from the envelope. Worth doing on large projects when you only need the rows. |
| `paginate` | string | No | Set to `cursor` to opt into keyset pagination. The envelope changes to `next_cursor` / `has_more` and drops `total_count`. Use it for deep or long-running traversals where offset pages would drift as rows are inserted. Follow the returned `next_cursor` as described in \[Pagination\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/pagination). |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed alongside the ids: `assignees` \(the assigned users\), `cycle` \(the cycle it belongs to\), `labels` \(the applied labels\), `modules` \(the modules it belongs to\), `parent` \(its parent work item\), `state` \(the work item's state object\), `type` \(its work item type\). Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `cursor` | string | No | Cursor from next_cursor. Repeat the original filters, per_page, and order_by when following it. |
| `pql` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Plane Query Language expression. Human-readable alternative to `filters`. Example: `priority = "urgent" AND assignee = currentUser\(\)`. Requires a Plane edition with work item query filtering; Community Edition rejects this parameter. |
| `filters` | json | No | v1 compatibility only. Structured filter expression. Supports nested `and`/`or`/`not` groups and field comparisons with operators like `__in`, `__gte`, `__range`, `__isnull`, `__icontains`, etc. JSON-encoded into the `filters=` query param by the client. Requires a Plane edition with work item query filtering; Community Edition rejects this parameter. |
| `assignee_id__isnull` | boolean | No | API v2: select records with a missing assignee when true, or a present assignee when false. |
| `label_id__isnull` | boolean | No | API v2: select records with a missing label when true, or a present label when false. |
| `parent_id__isnull` | boolean | No | API v2: select records with a missing parent when true, or a present parent when false. |
| `cycle_id__isnull` | boolean | No | API v2: select records with a missing cycle when true, or a present cycle when false. |
| `module_id__isnull` | boolean | No | API v2: select records with a missing module when true, or a present module when false. |

### Plane List work items across a workspace

List work items across a workspace in Plane. Requires API v2.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `assignee_id` | string | No | Filter by `assignee_id`. |
| `assignee_id__in` | json | No | Multiple values may be separated by commas. |
| `assignee_id__isnull` | boolean | No | Filter by `assignee_id__isnull`. |
| `count` | boolean | No | Set to false to skip the total_count COUNT\(\*\) \(omits total_count\). |
| `created_at__gte` | string | No | Filter by `created_at__gte`. |
| `created_at__lte` | string | No | Filter by `created_at__lte`. |
| `cycle_id` | string | No | Filter by `cycle_id`. |
| `cycle_id__in` | json | No | Multiple values may be separated by commas. |
| `cycle_id__isnull` | boolean | No | Filter by `cycle_id__isnull`. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Filter by `external_id`. |
| `external_source` | string | No | Filter by `external_source`. |
| `is_draft` | boolean | No | Filter by `is_draft`. |
| `label_id` | string | No | Filter by `label_id`. |
| `label_id__in` | json | No | Multiple values may be separated by commas. |
| `label_id__isnull` | boolean | No | Filter by `label_id__isnull`. |
| `module_id` | string | No | Filter by `module_id`. |
| `module_id__in` | json | No | Multiple values may be separated by commas. |
| `module_id__isnull` | boolean | No | Filter by `module_id__isnull`. |
| `offset` | number | No | Number of rows to skip from the start of the result set. |
| `order_by` | string | No | Field to order the list by. Prefix with '-' for descending \(e.g. '-created_at'\). Annotation-backed orders sort semantically and ride the default offset page. |
| `paginate` | string | No | Set to 'cursor' to opt into the COUNT-free keyset cursor envelope \(use for deep traversal\); omit for the default offset envelope with total_count. One of `cursor`. |
| `parent_id` | string | No | Filter by `parent_id`. |
| `parent_id__in` | json | No | Multiple values may be separated by commas. |
| `parent_id__isnull` | boolean | No | Filter by `parent_id__isnull`. |
| `per_page` | number | No | Page size \(max 200\). |
| `priority` | string | No | - `urgent` - Urgent - `high` - High - `medium` - Medium - `low` - Low - `none` - None One of `high`, `low`, `medium`, `none`, `urgent`. |
| `priority__in` | json | No | Multiple values may be separated by commas. - `urgent` - Urgent - `high` - High - `medium` - Medium - `low` - Low - `none` - None |
| `project_id` | string | No | Filter by `project_id`. |
| `project_id__in` | json | No | Multiple values may be separated by commas. |
| `search` | string | No | A search term. |
| `sequence_id` | number | No | Filter by `sequence_id`. |
| `start_date__gte` | string | No | Filter by `start_date__gte`. |
| `start_date__lte` | string | No | Filter by `start_date__lte`. |
| `state_group` | string | No | - `backlog` - Backlog - `unstarted` - Unstarted - `started` - Started - `completed` - Completed - `cancelled` - Cancelled - `triage` - Triage One of `backlog`, `cancelled`, `completed`, `started`, `triage`, `unstarted`. |
| `state_group__in` | json | No | Multiple values may be separated by commas. - `backlog` - Backlog - `unstarted` - Unstarted - `started` - Started - `completed` - Completed - `cancelled` - Cancelled - `triage` - Triage |
| `state_id` | string | No | Filter by `state_id`. |
| `state_id__in` | json | No | Multiple values may be separated by commas. |
| `target_date__gte` | string | No | Filter by `target_date__gte`. |
| `target_date__lte` | string | No | Filter by `target_date__lte`. |
| `type_id` | string | No | Filter by `type_id`. |
| `type_id__in` | json | No | Multiple values may be separated by commas. |
| `updated_at__gte` | string | No | Filter by `updated_at__gte`. |
| `updated_at__lte` | string | No | Filter by `updated_at__lte`. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from each row, not returned as `null`. `id` always comes back. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that names the valid set, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). Requestable here: `archived_at`, `assignee_ids`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `cycle_id`, `id`, `identifier`, `is_draft`, `label_ids`, `module_ids`, `name`, `parent_id`, `priority`, `project_id`, `sequence_id`, `start_date`, `state_id`, `target_date`, `type_id`. |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed: `assignees`, `cycle`, `labels`, `modules`, `parent`, `state`, `type`. Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `cursor` | string | No | Cursor from next_cursor. Repeat the original filters, per_page, and order_by when following it. |

### Plane Add or remove cycle work items

Add or remove cycle work items in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project the resource belongs to. Accepts the project UUID or its bare identifier, for example `ENG`. |
| `pk` | string | Yes | The cycle work item id. |
| `add` | json | No | The add. |
| `remove` | json | No | The remove. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 V2 Manage Cycle Work Itemsresult |

An unknown name is a `400`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). ### Plane Unarchive a work item

Unarchive a work item in Plane. Requires API v2.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project the work item belongs to. |
| `pk` | string | Yes | The work item's UUID. This lookup is UUID-only — a `PROJ-142` identifier here returns `404`. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `archived_at`, `assignee_ids`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `custom_fields`, `cycle_id`, `id`, `identifier`, `is_draft`, `label_ids`, `module_ids`, `name`, `parent_id`, `priority`, `project_id`, `sequence_id`, `start_date`, `state_id`, `target_date`, `type_id`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed alongside the ids: `assignees` \(the assigned users\), `cycle` \(the cycle it belongs to\), `labels` \(the applied labels\), `modules` \(the modules it belongs to\), `parent` \(its parent work item\), `state` \(the work item's state object\), `type` \(its work item type\). Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |

|   ↳ `custom_fields` | json | Custom fields |
|   ↳ `assignees` | array | Assignees |

### Plane Update a comment

Update a comment in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project the work item belongs to. |
| `work_item_id` | string | Yes | The work item the comment is attached to. |
| `pk` | string | Yes | The comment to update. |
| `comment_html` | string | No | Replacement body for the comment, as HTML. It overwrites the previous body — there is no append mode, so send the full new text. |
| `access` | string | No | Change the comment's visibility. - `INTERNAL` — visible to the project team - `EXTERNAL` — marked as visible outside the team, for example on a published project |
| `external_id` | string | No | Your system's identifier for this comment. Maximum 255 characters. Accepts `null` to clear the correlation. |
| `external_source` | string | No | The system `external_id` came from, for example `github` or `zendesk`. Maximum 255 characters. Accepts `null`. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `access`, `actor_id`, `comment_html`, `comment_stripped`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `edited_at`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `id`, `work_item_id`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed alongside the ids: `actor` \(the comment author\). Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `comment_json` | json | No | v1 compatibility only. Comment json. |

### Plane Update a cycle

Update a cycle in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project the cycle belongs to. |
| `pk` | string | Yes | The cycle to update. |
| `name` | string | No | New display name, unique within the project. Maximum 255 characters. Renaming onto a name another cycle already holds returns `409 conflict`. |
| `description` | string | No | Free-form description of what the cycle covers. |
| `start_date` | string | No | New opening date-time, in ISO 8601. Send `null` to unschedule the start. |
| `end_date` | string | No | New closing date-time, in ISO 8601. Send `null` to unschedule the end. |
| `timezone` | string | No | The IANA time zone the cycle's dates are interpreted in, for example `America/New_York` or `UTC`. Changing it re-anchors where the existing boundaries fall locally, so send it together with the dates when you are moving a cycle between regions. Any value outside the IANA list is rejected with `400 invalid_request`. |
| `sort_order` | number | No | Ordering weight for the cycle within the project. Lower values sort first when you list with `?order_by=sort_order`. |
| `logo_props` | json | No | JSON blob holding the cycle's icon configuration. Replaces the stored value outright — it is not merged key by key. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Your system's identifier for this cycle. Maximum 255 characters, nullable. |
| `external_source` | string | No | The system `external_id` came from, for example `jira`. Maximum 255 characters, nullable. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `description`, `end_date`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `id`, `logo_props`, `name`, `owned_by_id`, `sort_order`, `start_date`, `timezone`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed alongside the ids: `owned_by` \(the cycle owner\). Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `owned_by` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. User who owns the cycle. If not provided, defaults to the current user. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 Cycles |
|   ↳ `id` | json | Id |
|   ↳ `name` | json | Name |
|   ↳ `description` | json | Description |
|   ↳ `start_date` | string | Start date |
|   ↳ `end_date` | string | End date |
|   ↳ `timezone` | json | Timezone |
|   ↳ `owned_by_id` | string | Owned by id |
|   ↳ `sort_order` | json | Sort order |
|   ↳ `logo_props` | json | Logo props |
|   ↳ `external_id` | string | External id |
|   ↳ `external_source` | string | External source |
|   ↳ `created_at` | json | Created at |
|   ↳ `created_by_id` | string | Created by id |
|   ↳ `owned_by` | json | Owned by |
|   ↳ `total_issues` | json | Total issues |
|   ↳ `cancelled_issues` | json | Cancelled issues |
|   ↳ `completed_issues` | json | Completed issues |
|   ↳ `started_issues` | json | Started issues |
|   ↳ `unstarted_issues` | json | Unstarted issues |
|   ↳ `backlog_issues` | json | Backlog issues |

### Plane Update a label

Update a label in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project the label belongs to. |
| `pk` | string | Yes | The label id. |
| `name` | string | No | New display name, unique within the project. Maximum 255 characters. Renaming onto a name another label already holds returns `409 conflict`. |
| `color` | string | No | Hex color used wherever the label is rendered, for example `#e5484d`. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `description` | string | No | Free-form note about what the label is for. |
| `parent_id` | string | No | Move the label under a different parent. The new parent must be a label in the same project. Send `null` to make the label top-level again. |
| `sort_order` | number | No | Ordering weight within the project. Lower values sort first. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Your system's identifier for this label, for sync and import correlation. Maximum 255 characters. Send `null` to clear it. |
| `external_source` | string | No | The system `external_id` came from, for example `github` or `jira`. Maximum 255 characters. Send `null` to clear it. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `color`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `description`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `id`, `name`, `parent_id`, `sort_order`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 Labels |
|   ↳ `id` | json | Id |
|   ↳ `name` | json | Name |
|   ↳ `description` | json | Description |
|   ↳ `color` | json | Color |
|   ↳ `sort_order` | json | Sort order |
|   ↳ `parent_id` | string | Parent id |
|   ↳ `external_id` | string | External id |
|   ↳ `external_source` | string | External source |
|   ↳ `created_at` | json | Created at |
|   ↳ `created_by_id` | string | Created by id |
|   ↳ `updated_at` | string | Updated at |
|   ↳ `deleted_at` | string | Deleted at |
|   ↳ `created_by` | string | Created by |
|   ↳ `updated_by` | string | Updated by |
|   ↳ `workspace` | string | Workspace |
|   ↳ `project` | string | Project |
|   ↳ `parent` | string | Parent |
|   ↳ `deleted_by` | json | Deleted by |

### Plane Update a link

Update a link in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project the resource belongs to. Accepts the project UUID or its bare identifier, for example `ENG`. |
| `work_item_id` | string | Yes | The work item the resource hangs off. Accepts the work item UUID or its `PROJ-123` identifier. |
| `pk` | string | Yes | The link id. |
| `metadata` | json | No | JSON metadata object for the link. API v2 only. |
| `title` | string | No | Title. Maximum 255 characters. Nullable. |
| `url` | string | No | Target URL. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted**, not returned as `null`. `id` always comes back. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). Requestable here: `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `id`, `metadata`, `title`, `url`, `work_item_id`. |
| `issue_id` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Work item ID for the link. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 Work- Item- Links |
|   ↳ `created_at` | json | Created at |
|   ↳ `created_by_id` | string | Created by id |
|   ↳ `id` | json | Id |
|   ↳ `metadata` | json | Metadata |
|     ↳ `url` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `title` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `favicon` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `favicon_url` | json | Logo Props |
|   ↳ `title` | json | Title |
|   ↳ `url` | json | Url |
|   ↳ `work_item_id` | string | Work item id |
|   ↳ `updated_at` | string | Updated at |
|   ↳ `deleted_at` | string | Deleted at |
|   ↳ `created_by` | string | Created by |
|   ↳ `updated_by` | string | Updated by |
|   ↳ `project` | string | Project |
|   ↳ `workspace` | string | Workspace |
|   ↳ `issue` | string | Issue |
|   ↳ `deleted_by` | json | Deleted by |

### Plane Update a module

Update a module in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project the module belongs to. |
| `pk` | string | Yes | The module to update. |
| `name` | string | No | New display name, unique within the project. Maximum 255 characters. Renaming onto an existing module's name returns `409 conflict`. |
| `description` | string | No | Plain-text summary of what the module covers. |
| `status` | string | No | Move the module to a different lifecycle position. - `backlog` — Captured, not yet committed to - `planned` — Committed to but not started - `in-progress` — Actively being worked on - `paused` — Started, then put on hold - `completed` — Delivered - `cancelled` — Dropped without delivering Any value outside this list is a `400 invalid_request`. Transitions are unrestricted — a `completed` module can be sent back to `in-progress`. |
| `start_date` | string | No | Date the module is scheduled to begin, as `YYYY-MM-DD`. Send `null` to clear it. |
| `target_date` | string | No | Date the module is expected to land, as `YYYY-MM-DD`. Send `null` to clear it. Must not be earlier than the module's `start_date` — including the `start_date` already stored when you only send `target_date`. |
| `lead_id` | string | No | Reassign the module. Must be a member of this project — any other user id is rejected with a `400` naming `lead_id`. Send `null` to leave the module without a lead. |
| `sort_order` | number | No | Ordering weight used when modules are listed. Lower values sort first. |
| `logo_props` | json | No | Free-form JSON object holding the icon Plane renders for the module. The value you send replaces the stored object. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Your system's identifier for this module, for sync and import correlation. Maximum 255 characters. Nullable. |
| `external_source` | string | No | The system `external_id` came from, for example `github` or `jira`. Maximum 255 characters. Nullable. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `archived_at`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `description`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `id`, `lead_id`, `logo_props`, `member_ids`, `name`, `sort_order`, `start_date`, `status`, `target_date`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed alongside the ids: `lead` \(the module lead\), `members` \(the module members\). Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `members` | json | No | v1 compatibility only. Members. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 Modules |
|   ↳ `id` | json | Id |
|   ↳ `name` | json | Name |
|   ↳ `description` | json | Description |
|   ↳ `status` | json | Status |
|   ↳ `start_date` | string | Start date |
|   ↳ `target_date` | string | Target date |
|   ↳ `lead_id` | string | Lead id |
|   ↳ `member_ids` | array | Member ids |
|   ↳ `sort_order` | json | Sort order |
|   ↳ `logo_props` | json | Logo props |
|   ↳ `external_id` | string | External id |
|   ↳ `external_source` | string | External source |
|   ↳ `archived_at` | string | Archived at |
|   ↳ `created_at` | json | Created at |
|   ↳ `created_by_id` | string | Created by id |
|   ↳ `lead` | json | Lead |
|   ↳ `members` | array | Members |
|   ↳ `total_issues` | json | Total issues |
|   ↳ `cancelled_issues` | json | Cancelled issues |
|   ↳ `completed_issues` | json | Completed issues |
|   ↳ `started_issues` | json | Started issues |
|   ↳ `unstarted_issues` | json | Unstarted issues |
|   ↳ `backlog_issues` | json | Backlog issues |
|   ↳ `updated_at` | string | Updated at |
|   ↳ `deleted_at` | string | Deleted at |
|   ↳ `description_text` | json | Description text |
|   ↳ `description_html` | json | Description html |
|   ↳ `view_props` | json | View props |
|   ↳ `created_by` | string | Created by |
|   ↳ `updated_by` | string | Updated by |
|   ↳ `project` | string | Project |
|   ↳ `workspace` | string | Workspace |
|   ↳ `deleted_by` | json | Deleted by |

### Plane Update a project

Update a project in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `pk` | string | Yes | The project id. |
| `archive_in` | number | No | The archive in. |
| `close_in` | number | No | The close in. |
| `cover_image` | string | No | URL of the cover image. Nullable. |
| `cycle_view` | boolean | No | Whether cycle view. |
| `default_assignee_id` | string | No | Id of the related default assignee. Nullable. |
| `default_state_id` | string | No | Id of the related default state. Nullable. |
| `description` | string | No | Free-form description. |
| `emoji` | string | No | Emoji shown alongside the name. Maximum 255 characters. Nullable. |
| `estimate_id` | string | No | Id of the related estimate. Nullable. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Your system's identifier for this record, for sync and import correlation. Maximum 255 characters. Nullable. |

| `guest_view_all_features` | boolean | No | Whether guest view all features. |

| `identifier` | string | No | Short project key used to prefix work item numbers, for example `ENG` in `ENG-142`. Maximum 255 characters. |

| `is_issue_type_enabled` | boolean | No | Whether is issue type enabled. |

| `network` | string | No | Project visibility: `0` is private to members, `2` is visible to the whole workspace. |

| `start_date` | string | No | Planned start date, as `YYYY-MM-DD`. Nullable. |
| `state_id` | string | No | Id of the related state. Nullable. |

| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted**, not returned as `null`. `id` always comes back. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). Requestable here: `archive_in`, `archived_at`, `close_in`, `cover_image`, `cover_image_url`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `cycle_view`, `default_assignee_id`, `default_state_id`, `description`, `emoji`, `estimate_id`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `guest_view_all_features`, `icon_prop`, `id`, `identifier`, `intake_view`, `is_issue_type_enabled`, `is_time_tracking_enabled`, `issue_views_view`, `logo_props`, `module_view`, `name`, `network`, `page_view`, `priority`, `project_lead_id`, `start_date`, `state_id`, `target_date`, `timezone`. |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed: `default_assignee`, `project_lead`. Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `v1_icon_prop` | json | No | v1 compatibility only. Icon prop. |

### Plane Update a project page

Update a project page in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project the resource belongs to. Accepts the project UUID or its bare identifier, for example `ENG`. |
| `pk` | string | Yes | The project page id. |
| `access` | string | No | Who can see this. |
| `archived_at` | string | No | The archived at. Nullable. |
| `collection_id` | string | No | Id of the related collection. Nullable. |
| `color` | string | No | Hex color used wherever this is rendered, for example `#3f76ff`. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `description_html` | string | No | Rich-text body as HTML. This is the field the Plane editor round-trips. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Your system's identifier for this record, for sync and import correlation. Maximum 255 characters. Nullable. |
| `external_source` | string | No | The system `external_id` came from, for example `github` or `jira`. Maximum 255 characters. Nullable. |
| `is_locked` | boolean | No | Prevents further edits to the content. |
| `logo_props` | string | No | Editor-owned logo descriptor. Pass back what you read rather than composing it by hand. |
| `name` | string | No | Display name. |
| `parent_id` | string | No | Id of the related parent. Nullable. |
| `sort_order` | number | No | Manual ordering weight. Lower sorts first. |
| `view_props` | string | No | Editor-owned layout descriptor. Pass back what you read. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted**, not returned as `null`. `id` always comes back. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). Requestable here: `access`, `archived_at`, `collection_id`, `color`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `description_html`, `description_stripped`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `id`, `is_global`, `is_locked`, `logo_props`, `name`, `owned_by_id`, `parent_id`, `sort_order`, `view_props`. |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed: `owned_by`, `parent`. Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `v1_access` | number | No | v1 compatibility only. Access |
| `v1_view_props` | json | No | v1 compatibility only. View props |
| `v1_logo_props` | json | No | v1 compatibility only. Logo props |

### Plane Update a state

Update a state in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project the state belongs to. |
| `pk` | string | Yes | The id of the state to update. |
| `name` | string | No | New display name, unique within the project. Maximum 255 characters. Renaming is safe for reporting: boards and charts key off `group`, not `name`. |
| `color` | string | No | Hex color used wherever the state is rendered, for example `#3f76ff`. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `description` | string | No | Free-form description of what the state means in this workflow. |
| `group` | string | No | Move the state to a different workflow group. This changes how every work item in the state is counted by boards, charts, and cycle progress, so switching a state from `started` to `completed` retroactively changes what those work items report as. - `backlog` — Not yet scheduled - `unstarted` — Scheduled but not begun - `started` — Actively in progress - `completed` — Finished successfully - `cancelled` — Closed without completion - `triage` — Awaiting intake review |
| `sequence` | number | No | Ordering weight within the project. Lower values sort first. Set it to reposition the state in the workflow. |
| `is_default` | boolean | No | Make this the project's default state — where work items land when no `state_id` is supplied. A project has exactly one default, so setting this to `true` clears the flag on the state that held it. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Your system's identifier for this state, for sync and import correlation. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `external_source` | string | No | The system `external_id` came from, for example `github` or `jira`. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `color`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `description`, `external_id`, `external_source`, `group`, `id`, `is_default`, `is_triage`, `name`, `sequence`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `is_triage` | boolean | No | v1 compatibility only. Is triage. |
| `default` | boolean | No | v1 compatibility only. Default. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 States |
|   ↳ `id` | json | Id |
|   ↳ `name` | json | Name |
|   ↳ `description` | json | Description |
|   ↳ `color` | json | Color |
|   ↳ `group` | json | Group |
|   ↳ `sequence` | json | Sequence |
|   ↳ `is_default` | boolean | Is default |
|   ↳ `is_triage` | json | Is triage |
|   ↳ `external_id` | string | External id |
|   ↳ `external_source` | string | External source |
|   ↳ `created_at` | json | Created at |
|   ↳ `created_by_id` | string | Created by id |
|   ↳ `updated_at` | string | Updated at |
|   ↳ `deleted_at` | string | Deleted at |
|   ↳ `default` | boolean | Default |
|   ↳ `created_by` | string | Created by |
|   ↳ `updated_by` | string | Updated by |
|   ↳ `project` | string | Project |
|   ↳ `workspace` | string | Workspace |
|   ↳ `slug` | string | Slug |
|   ↳ `deleted_by` | json | Deleted by |

### Plane Update a webhook

Update a webhook in Plane. Requires API v2.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `pk` | string | Yes | The webhook id. |
| `content_type` | string | No | - `application/json` - application/json - `application/x-www-form-urlencoded` - application/x-www-form-urlencoded One of `application/json`, `application/x-www-form-urlencoded`. |
| `is_active` | boolean | No | Whether the record is active. |
| `name` | string | No | Display name. Maximum 255 characters. Nullable. |
| `scopes` | json | No | The scopes. |
| `url` | string | No | Target URL. |
| `version` | string | No | Version string, for example `1.4.0`. Maximum 10 characters. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted**, not returned as `null`. `id` always comes back. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). Requestable here: `content_type`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `id`, `is_active`, `name`, `scopes`, `url`, `version`. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 Webhooks |
|   ↳ `content_type` | string | Content type |
|   ↳ `created_at` | string | Created at |
|   ↳ `created_by_id` | string | Created by id |
|   ↳ `id` | string | Unique identifier. |
|   ↳ `is_active` | boolean | Is active |
|   ↳ `name` | string | Name |
|   ↳ `scopes` | array | Scopes |
|   ↳ `url` | string | Url |
|   ↳ `version` | string | Version |

### Plane Update a work item

Update a work item in Plane. Supports API v1 compatibility.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Plane personal access token from Profile → API tokens. |
| `baseUrl` | string | No | Plane instance origin, optionally with a reverse-proxy path prefix. Defaults to https://api.plane.so. Do not include /api/v1 or /api/v2. |
| `apiVersion` | string | No | API version: v2 \(default\) for Plane Cloud and current commercial instances; v1 for Community Edition or older self-hosted instances. Unsupported operations or fields fail explicitly. |
| `bodyOverrides` | json | No | JSON overrides using the canonical input names for the selected API version. Preserves empty strings, null, and empty arrays; unsupported fields are rejected. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | Yes | Workspace slug from the Plane URL \(for example, my-team\). |
| `project_id` | string | Yes | The project the work item belongs to. |
| `pk` | string | Yes | The work item's UUID. This lookup is UUID-only — a `PROJ-142` identifier here returns `404`. |
| `name` | string | No | New title. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `description_html` | string | No | Replacement rich-text body as HTML. Sanitized on the way in; content that can't be sanitized is rejected with a `400`. Not part of the read shape, so it won't appear in the response. |
| `priority` | string | No | One of `urgent`, `high`, `medium`, `low`, `none`. |
| `state_id` | string | No | Move the work item to another state of the same project. This is the field workflow rules police — see \[Workflow rules\]\(#workflow-rules-can-reject-a-transition\). |
| `state` | string | No | The target state's **name** instead of its id, for example `Done`. Matched case-insensitively within the project. Write-only. Sending both `state` and `state_id` is a `400`. |
| `type_id` | string | No | Change the work item type. Send `null` to make the work item untyped. |
| `type` | string | No | The type's **name** instead of its id, for example `Bug`. Write-only. |
| `parent_id` | string | No | Re-parent the work item. The parent must be in the same workspace and may be in a different project. Send `null` to detach it and make it top-level. |
| `parent` | string | No | The parent's **identifier** instead of its id, for example `PROJ-118`. Write-only. |
| `assignee_ids` | json | No | **Replaces** the whole assignee set — it is not additive. Send the complete list you want, `\[\]` to unassign everyone, or omit the field to leave assignees untouched. |
| `assignees` | json | No | Member **email addresses** instead of ids. Same replace-the-set semantics. Every address must belong to an active, assignable project member. Write-only. |
| `label_ids` | json | No | **Replaces** the whole label set. Send `\[\]` to strip all labels, or omit the field to leave them untouched. |
| `labels` | json | No | Label **names** instead of ids. Same replace-the-set semantics. A name that exists at both project and workspace level is ambiguous and returns a `400` telling you to use `label_ids`. Write-only. |
| `estimate_point_id` | string | No | Change the estimate point, from the project's active estimate system. Send `null` to clear the estimate. |
| `estimate` | string | No | The estimate point's **value** instead of its id, for example `5`. Write-only. |
| `start_date` | string | No | Planned start, or `null` to clear it. Must not be after `target_date`. |
| `target_date` | string | No | Planned due date, or `null` to clear it. |
| `external_id` | string | No | Your system's identifier for this work item. Maximum 255 characters. Filterable on \[List work items\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/work-items/list-work-items), but not returned on reads. |
| `external_source` | string | No | The system `external_id` came from, for example `github` or `jira`. Maximum 255 characters. |
| `fields` | string | No | Comma-separated list of fields to return. Unrequested keys are **omitted** from the response, not returned as `null`, so absent means "not requested" and `null` means "actually null". `id` always comes back whether or not you name it. Pass `all` for every requestable field. An unknown name is a `400` that lists the valid set and suggests the closest match, so a typo can't silently cost you the saving. Requestable here: `archived_at`, `assignee_ids`, `created_at`, `created_by_id`, `custom_fields`, `cycle_id`, `id`, `identifier`, `is_draft`, `label_ids`, `module_ids`, `name`, `parent_id`, `priority`, `project_id`, `sequence_id`, `start_date`, `state_id`, `target_date`, `type_id`. See \[Sparse fields\]\(https://developers.plane.so/api-reference/v2/sparse-fields). |
| `expand` | string | No | Comma-separated relations to embed alongside the ids: `assignees` \(the assigned users\), `cycle` \(the cycle it belongs to\), `labels` \(the applied labels\), `modules` \(the modules it belongs to\), `parent` \(its parent work item\), `state` \(the work item's state object\), `type` \(its work item type\). Expanded relations appear beside their ID fields and survive sparse field filtering. Use only the relation names listed above. |
| `deleted_at` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Deleted at. |
| `point` | number | No | v1 compatibility only. Point. |
| `sequence_id` | number | No | v1 compatibility only. Sequence id. |
| `sort_order` | number | No | v1 compatibility only. Sort order. |
| `archived_at` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Archived at. |
| `is_draft` | boolean | No | v1 compatibility only. Is draft. |
| `created_by` | string | No | v1 compatibility only. Created by. |
| `custom_fields` | json | No | JSON custom field values. API v2 only. |
| `cycle_id` | string | No | Cycle ID. API v2 only. |
| `module_ids` | json | No | Module IDs. API v2 only. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `result` | object | Plane V2 Work- Items |
|   ↳ `id` | json | Id |
|   ↳ `name` | json | Name |
|   ↳ `identifier` | string | Identifier |
|   ↳ `sequence_id` | json | Sequence id |
|   ↳ `priority` | json | Priority |
|   ↳ `state_id` | string | State id |

|   ↳ `assignee_ids` | array | Assignee ids |
|   ↳ `label_ids` | array | Label ids |
|   ↳ `parent_id` | string | Parent id |

|   ↳ `target_date` | string | Target date |
|   ↳ `is_draft` | json | Is draft |
|   ↳ `archived_at` | string | Archived at |

|   ↳ `created_by_id` | string | Created by id |
|   ↳ `custom_fields` | json | Custom fields |

|     ↳ `external_id` | json | Id |
|     ↳ `created_by` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `updated_by` | json | Id |
|     ↳ `workspace` | string | Asset Id |

|     ↳ `parent` | json | Id |
|   ↳ `modules` | array | Modules |
|   ↳ `parent` | json | Parent |
|     ↳ `id` | string | Asset Id |
|     ↳ `name` | string | Asset Id |

## Triggers

A **Trigger** is a block that starts a workflow when an event happens in this service.

### Plane All Events

Trigger a workflow for any signed Plane event.

#### Configuration

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `autoRegister` | boolean | No | Requires the Plane v2 webhook API. Use manual setup for Community Edition. |
| `triggerApiKey` | string | Yes | API Token |
| `triggerBaseUrl` | string | No | Instance URL |
| `triggerWorkspaceSlug` | string | Yes | Workspace Slug |
| `webhookSecret` | string | Yes | Webhook Secret |
| `projectId` | string | No | Self-hosted v1 deletions without project information are skipped when this filter is set. |
| `workspaceId` | string | No | Workspace ID |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `version` | string | Payload version: v1 or v2. |
| `event` | string | Original Plane event or v1 resource name. |
| `eventName` | string | Normalized event name, such as workitem.updated. |
| `action` | string | v1 action; null for v2. |
| `event_id` | string | v2 event ID, stable across retries; null for v1. |
| `delivery_id` | string | Delivery attempt ID from the payload or X-Plane-Delivery header. |
| `entity_id` | string | v2 primary entity ID; null for v1. |
| `entity_type` | string | v2 entity type; null for v1. |
| `webhook_id` | string | Plane webhook configuration ID. |
| `workspace_id` | string | Plane workspace ID. |
| `workspace_slug` | string | v1 workspace slug; null for v2. |
| `data` | json | Complete entity payload, including resource-specific fields. v2 deletions send an empty object. |
| `activity` | json | v1 changed field, old/new values, actor and identifiers; null for v2. |
| `previous_attributes` | json | v2 previous changed values or full deleted record; null for v1. |


---
